\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00019}00019\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ PURPOSE}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00020}00020\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ This\ program\ generates\ the\ fort.10\ file\ for\ TurboRVB,\ which\ contains\ all\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00021}00021\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ information\ about\ the\ wave\ function\ (Slater\ determinant,\ Pfaffian,\ Jastrow,}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00022}00022\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ symmetry,\ atomic\ positions,\ etc.)\ for\ both\ periodic\ (solid)\ and\ open\ systems.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00023}00023\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ The\ fort.10\ file\ is\ a\ key\ input\ for\ quantum\ Monte\ Carlo\ (QMC)\ calculations.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00024}00024\ \textcolor{comment}{!}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00025}00025\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ MAIN\ FEATURES}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00026}00026\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Supports\ periodic\ boundary\ conditions\ (PBC)\ and\ open\ boundary\ conditions.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00027}00027\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Handles\ arbitrary\ crystal\ structures,\ including\ tilted\ cells.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00028}00028\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Automatically\ detects\ and\ applies\ point\ group\ and\ translation\ symmetries.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00029}00029\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Supports\ Slater,\ Pfaffian,\ and\ Jastrow\ wave\ functions\ (1-\/,\ 2-\/,\ 3-\/body).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00030}00030\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Can\ read\ atomic\ wave\ functions\ from\ file\ or\ generate\ them\ from\ input.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00031}00031\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Outputs\ auxiliary\ files\ (e.g.,\ structure.xsf,\ symmetries.dat)\ for\ analysis.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00032}00032\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ -\/\ Flexible\ input\ via\ Fortran\ namelists\ for\ system,\ electrons,\ and\ symmetries.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00033}00033\ \textcolor{comment}{!}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00034}00034\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ USAGE}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00036}00036\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ \ \ \ \&system,\ \&electrons,\ \&symmetries,\ and\ ATOMIC\_POSITIONS\ (and\ optionally}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00037}00037\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ \ \ \ ATOMIC\_SPECIES,\ UNPAIRED,\ etc.).}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00039}00039\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ ./makefort10\ <\ makefort10.input}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{makefort10_8f90_source_l00040}00040\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ \ \ 3.\ The\ program\ will\ generate\ fort.10\_new\ (and\ structure.xsf,\ symmetries.dat).}}
